## About Laravel

Laravel is a web application framework with expressive, elegant syntax. We believe development must be an enjoyable and creative experience to be truly fulfilling. Laravel takes the pain out of development by easing common tasks used in many web projects, such as:

- [Simple, fast routing engine](https://laravel.com/docs/routing).
- [Powerful dependency injection container](https://laravel.com/docs/container).

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\DashboardController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class DashboardController extends Controller
{
    public function __invoke(Request $request): View
    {
        $user = $request->user();
        $user->loadMissing('vaiTro', 'toChuc');

        $role = $user->vaiTro?->ma_vai_tro;
        $orgId = $user->to_chuc_id;

        $stats = $this->statsForRole($role, $user->getAuthIdentifier(), $orgId);

        return view('dashboard', [
            'user' => $user,
            'role' => $role,
            'navItems' => config('role_menu.' . $role, []),
            'stats' => $stats['cards'] ?? [],
            'alerts' => $stats['alerts'] ?? [],
        ]);
    }

    private function statsForRole(?string $role, int $accountId, mixed $orgId): array
    {
        return match ($role) {
            'ADMIN' => $this->adminStats(),
            'NGUOI_SAN_XUAT' => $this->producerStats($orgId),
            'NV_SO_CHE_DONG_GOI' => $this->processingStats($orgId),
            'QC' => $this->qcStats(),
            'NV_KHO_LANH' => $this->warehouseStats(),
            'TAI_XE' => $this->driverStats($accountId),
            'QUAN_LY_CHUOI' => $this->chainStats(),
            default => ['cards' => [], 'alerts' => []],
        };
    }

    private function card(string $label, mixed $value, string $hint = ''): array
    {
        return compact('label', 'value', 'hint');
    }

    private function adminStats(): array
    {
        $accounts = DB::table('tai_khoan_he_thong')->count();
        $orgs = DB::table('to_chuc')->count();
        $locked = DB::table('tai_khoan_he_thong')->where('trang_thai', 'KHOA')->count();

        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('Tá»•ng tĂ i khoáº£n', $accounts),
                $this->card('Tá»•ng tá»• chá»©c', $orgs),
                $this->card('TĂ i khoáº£n bá»‹ khĂ³a', $locked),
            ],
            'alerts' => $locked > 0
                ? ["CĂ³ {$locked} tĂ i khoáº£n Ä‘ang bá»‹ khĂ³a cáº§n xá»­ lĂ½."]
                : [],
        ];
    }

    private function producerStats(mixed $orgId): array
    {
        $plots = DB::table('vung_trong')->when($orgId, fn ($q) => $q->where('to_chuc_id', $orgId));
        $plotIds = (clone $plots)->pluck('vung_trong_id');
        $seasons = DB::table('mua_vu')->whereIn('vung_trong_id', $plotIds);
        $seasonIds = (clone $seasons)->pluck('mua_vu_id');
        $logs = $seasonIds->isNotEmpty()
            ? DB::table('nhat_ky_canh_tac')->whereIn('mua_vu_id', $seasonIds)->count()
            : 0;

        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('VĂ¹ng trá»“ng', (clone $plots)->count()),
                $this->card('MĂ¹a vá»¥', (clone $seasons)->count()),
                $this->card('Nháº­t kĂ½ canh tĂ¡c', $logs),
            ],
            'alerts' => [],
        ];
    }

    private function processingStats(mixed $orgId): array
    {
        $lots = DB::table('lo_nong_san')->when($orgId, fn ($q) => $q->where('to_chuc_nguon_id', $orgId));
        $packing = DB::table('don_vi_dong_goi_sscc')->count();

        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('LĂ´ Ä‘Ă£ sÆ¡ cháº¿', (clone $lots)->where('trang_thai', 'DA_SO_CHE')->count()),
                $this->card('Tá»•ng lĂ´ nguá»“n', (clone $lots)->count()),
                $this->card('ÄÆ¡n vá»‹ Ä‘Ă³ng gĂ³i SSCC', $packing),
            ],
            'alerts' => [],
        ];
    }

    private function qcStats(): array
    {
        $pending = DB::table('phieu_kiem_dinh')->where('ket_luan', 'CHO_KIEM_DINH')->count();
        $pass = DB::table('phieu_kiem_dinh')->where('ket_luan', 'DAT')->count();
        $fail = DB::table('phieu_kiem_dinh')->where('ket_luan', 'KHONG_DAT')->count();

        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('LĂ´ chá» kiá»ƒm Ä‘á»‹nh', $pending),
                $this->card('Káº¿t luáº­n Ä‘áº¡t', $pass),
                $this->card('Káº¿t luáº­n khĂ´ng Ä‘áº¡t', $fail),
            ],
            'alerts' => $pending > 0 ? ["CĂ³ {$pending} phiáº¿u Ä‘ang chá» kiá»ƒm Ä‘á»‹nh."] : [],
        ];
    }

    private function warehouseStats(): array
    {
        $inStock = DB::table('lo_nong_san')->where('trang_thai', 'TRONG_KHO')->count();
        $warehouses = DB::table('kho_lanh')->count();
        $unhandled = DB::table('canh_bao_chuoi_lanh')->where('trang_thai', 'CHUA_XU_LY')->count();

        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('LĂ´ trong kho', $inStock),
                $this->card('Kho láº¡nh', $warehouses),
                $this->card('Cáº£nh bĂ¡o chÆ°a xá»­ lĂ½', $unhandled),
            ],
            'alerts' => $unhandled > 0 ? ["CĂ³ {$unhandled} cáº£nh bĂ¡o chuá»—i láº¡nh chÆ°a xá»­ lĂ½."] : [],
        ];
    }

    private function driverStats(int $accountId): array
    {
        $trips = DB::table('chuyen_van_chuyen')->where('tai_xe_id', $accountId);
        $running = (clone $trips)->where('trang_thai', 'DANG_VAN_CHUYEN')->count();
        $done = (clone $trips)->where('trang_thai', 'HOAN_THANH')->count();

        if ((clone $trips)->count() === 0) {
            $trips = DB::table('chuyen_van_chuyen');
            $running = (clone $trips)->where('trang_thai', 'DANG_VAN_CHUYEN')->count();
            $done = (clone $trips)->where('trang_thai', 'HOAN_THANH')->count();
        }

        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('Chuyáº¿n Ä‘ang váº­n chuyá»ƒn', $running),
                $this->card('Chuyáº¿n hoĂ n thĂ nh', $done),
                $this->card('Phiáº¿u giao hĂ ng', DB::table('phieu_giao_hang')->count()),
            ],
            'alerts' => [],
        ];
    }

    private function chainStats(): array
    {
        return [
            'cards' => [
                $this->card('Tá»•ng lĂ´ nĂ´ng sáº£n', DB::table('lo_nong_san')->count()),
                $this->card('Chuyáº¿n váº­n chuyá»ƒn', DB::table('chuyen_van_chuyen')->count()),
                $this->card('Cáº£nh bĂ¡o chÆ°a xá»­ lĂ½', DB::table('canh_bao_chuoi_lanh')->where('trang_thai', 'CHUA_XU_LY')->count()),
                $this->card('Thiáº¿t bá»‹ IoT', DB::table('thiet_bi_iot')->count()),
            ],
            'alerts' => [],
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Services\OrganizationRegistrationService;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class OrganizationRegistrationController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private OrganizationRegistrationService $service)
    {
    }

    public function showForm(): View
    {
        return view('organization-registration.create');
    }

    public function submit(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'organization_type' => ['required', 'in:NONG_HO,HTX,DOANH_NGHIEP_NONG_NGHIEP'],
            'organization_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:200'],
            'representative_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:150'],
            'legal_id' => ['required', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'contact_phone' => ['required', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'contact_email' => ['required', 'email', 'max:254'],
            'organization_address' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'cultivation_region' => ['required', 'string', 'max:500'],
            'legal_documents' => ['required', 'array', 'min:1'],
            'legal_documents.*' => ['file', 'mimes:pdf,jpg,jpeg,png', 'max:5120'],
            'certification_documents' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'certification_documents.*' => ['file', 'mimes:pdf,jpg,jpeg,png', 'max:5120'],
            'cold_chain_items' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'cold_chain_items.*.name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'cold_chain_items.*.type' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:80'],
            'cold_chain_items.*.identifier' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'cold_chain_items.*.description' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
            'sensors' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'sensors.*.name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'sensors.*.type' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:80'],
            'sensors.*.serial' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'sensors.*.description' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'organization_type.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng chá»n loáº¡i hĂ¬nh cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ.',
            'organization_name.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p tĂªn cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ.',
            'representative_name.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p ngÆ°á»i Ä‘áº¡i diá»‡n.',
            'legal_id.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p CCCD hoáº·c mĂ£ sá»‘ thuáº¿.',
            'contact_phone.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i.',
            'contact_email.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p email liĂªn há»‡.',
            'organization_address.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ.',
            'cultivation_region.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p thĂ´ng tin vĂ¹ng trá»“ng.',
            'legal_documents.required' => 'Cáº§n táº£i lĂªn Ă­t nháº¥t má»™t giáº¥y tá» phĂ¡p lĂ½.',
            'legal_documents.*.mimes' => 'Chá»‰ cháº¥p nháº­n file PDF, JPG, JPEG hoáº·c PNG.',
            'legal_documents.*.max' => 'Má»—i file tá»‘i Ä‘a 5MB.',
            'certification_documents.*.mimes' => 'Chá»‰ cháº¥p nháº­n file PDF, JPG, JPEG hoáº·c PNG.',
            'certification_documents.*.max' => 'Má»—i file tá»‘i Ä‘a 5MB.',
        ]);

        $validated['legal_documents'] = $request->file('legal_documents', []);
        $validated['certification_documents'] = $request->file('certification_documents', []);

        $created = $this->service->create($validated);

        return redirect()
            ->route('organization-registration.show', ['applicationCode' => $created['application_code']])
            ->with('status', 'Há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Äƒng kĂ½ Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»­i thĂ nh cĂ´ng.');
    }

    public function success(string $applicationCode): View
    {
        $registration = $this->service->findByCode($applicationCode);

        abort_unless($registration, 404);

        return view('organization-registration.success', [
            'registration' => $registration,
        ]);
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\AccountController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\TaiKhoanHeThong;
use App\Models\VaiTro;
use App\Support\AccountStatus;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class AccountController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): View
    {
        $query = TaiKhoanHeThong::query()->with(['vaiTro', 'toChuc']);

        if ($request->filled('q')) {
            $q = trim($request->string('q')->toString());
            $query->where(function ($w) use ($q) {
                $w->where('ho_ten', 'like', "%{$q}%")
                    ->orWhere('email', 'like', "%{$q}%");
            });
        }

        if ($request->filled('trang_thai')) {
            $query->where('trang_thai', $request->string('trang_thai')->toString());
        }

        if ($request->filled('vai_tro_id')) {
            $query->where('vai_tro_id', (int) $request->input('vai_tro_id'));
        }

        $accounts = $query->orderByDesc('tai_khoan_id')->paginate(20)->withQueryString();

        return view('admin.accounts.index', [
            'accounts' => $accounts,
            'roles' => VaiTro::orderBy('vai_tro_id')->get(),
            'statuses' => [
                AccountStatus::DANG_HOAT_DONG => 'Äang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng',
                AccountStatus::CHO_KICH_HOAT => 'Chá» kĂ­ch hoáº¡t',
                AccountStatus::KHOA => 'Bá»‹ khĂ³a',
            ],
            'filters' => $request->only(['q', 'trang_thai', 'vai_tro_id']),
        ]);
    }

    public function lock(Request $request, int $id): RedirectResponse
    {
        $account = TaiKhoanHeThong::findOrFail($id);

        if ($account->getAuthIdentifier() === $request->user()->getAuthIdentifier()) {
            return back()->withErrors(['account' => 'KhĂ´ng thá»ƒ khĂ³a chĂ­nh tĂ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n.']);
        }

        $account->update(['trang_thai' => AccountStatus::KHOA]);

        return back()->with('status', "ÄĂ£ khĂ³a tĂ i khoáº£n {$account->email}.");
    }

    public function unlock(int $id): RedirectResponse
    {
        $account = TaiKhoanHeThong::findOrFail($id);
        $account->update(['trang_thai' => AccountStatus::DANG_HOAT_DONG]);

        return back()->with('status', "ÄĂ£ má»Ÿ khĂ³a tĂ i khoáº£n {$account->email}.");
    }

    public function changeRole(Request $request, int $id): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'vai_tro_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:vai_tro,vai_tro_id'],
        ], [
            'vai_tro_id.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng chá»n vai trĂ².',
        ]);

        $account = TaiKhoanHeThong::findOrFail($id);
        $role = VaiTro::findOrFail($validated['vai_tro_id']);

        if ($role->ma_vai_tro === 'ADMIN') {
            return back()->withErrors(['vai_tro_id' => 'KhĂ´ng thá»ƒ gĂ¡n vai trĂ² quáº£n trá»‹ viĂªn táº¡i Ä‘Ă¢y.']);
        }

        $account->update(['vai_tro_id' => $validated['vai_tro_id']]);

        return back()->with('status', "ÄĂ£ chuyá»ƒn {$account->email} sang vai trĂ² {$role->ten_vai_tro}.");
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\AccountProvisionController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\TaiKhoanHeThong;
use App\Models\VaiTro;
use App\Services\OrganizationRegistrationService;
use App\Support\AccountStatus;
use App\Support\InitialPasswordStore;
use App\Support\RegistrationStatuses;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class AccountProvisionController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(
        private OrganizationRegistrationService $registrations,
        private InitialPasswordStore $initialPasswordStore
    ) {
    }

    public function provision(Request $request, string $applicationCode): RedirectResponse
    {
        $registration = $this->registrations->findByCode($applicationCode);
        abort_unless($registration, 404);

        if (($registration['status'] ?? null) !== RegistrationStatuses::DA_PHE_DUYET) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'provision' => 'Chá»‰ cĂ³ thá»ƒ cáº¥p tĂ i khoáº£n cho há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Ă£ phĂª duyá»‡t.',
            ]);
        }

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'full_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:150'],
            'email' => ['required', 'email', 'max:254'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'vai_tro_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:vai_tro,vai_tro_id'],
        ], [
            'full_name.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p há» tĂªn ngÆ°á»i Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥p tĂ i khoáº£n.',
            'email.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p email tĂ i khoáº£n.',
            'vai_tro_id.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng chá»n vai trĂ².',
        ]);

        $role = VaiTro::findOrFail($validated['vai_tro_id']);

        if ($role->ma_vai_tro === 'ADMIN') {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'vai_tro_id' => 'KhĂ´ng cho phĂ©p cáº¥p vai trĂ² quáº£n trá»‹ viĂªn tá»« luá»“ng Ä‘Äƒng kĂ½ cĂ´ng khai.',
            ]);
        }

        $existing = TaiKhoanHeThong::where('email', $validated['email'])->first();
        if ($existing) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'email' => 'Email nĂ y Ä‘Ă£ tá»“n táº¡i trong há»‡ thá»‘ng. KhĂ´ng thá»ƒ cáº¥p trĂ¹ng tĂ i khoáº£n.',
            ]);
        }

        $initialPasswordPlain = Str::random(14).'!aA1';

        $created = DB::transaction(function () use ($registration, $validated, $initialPasswordPlain) {
            $account = TaiKhoanHeThong::create([
                'vai_tro_id' => $validated['vai_tro_id'],
                'to_chuc_id' => $registration['organization_id'],
                'ho_ten' => $validated['full_name'],
                'email' => $validated['email'],
                'mat_khau_hash' => Hash::make($initialPasswordPlain),
                'so_dien_thoai' => $validated['phone'] ?: null,
                'trang_thai' => AccountStatus::DANG_HOAT_DONG,
            ]);

            return $account;
        });

        $this->initialPasswordStore->markForceChange((int) $created->tai_khoan_id);
        $this->registrations->markProvisioned($applicationCode, (int) $created->tai_khoan_id);

        $mailStatus = 'SIMULATED';
        $mailMessage = 'Há»‡ thá»‘ng Ä‘ang á»Ÿ cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ phĂ¡t triá»ƒn: Ä‘Ă£ lÆ°u log thĂ´ng tin cáº¥p tĂ i khoáº£n, chÆ°a gá»­i email thá»±c táº¿.';

        if (config('mail.default') !== 'log') {
            Mail::raw(
                "TĂ i khoáº£n Farm2Fork cá»§a báº¡n Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥p.\nEmail: {$created->email}\nMáº­t kháº©u táº¡m thá»i Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥p bá»Ÿi quáº£n trá»‹ viĂªn. Vui lĂ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p vĂ  Ä‘á»•i máº­t kháº©u ngay.",
                function ($message) use ($created) {
                    $message->to($created->email)
                        ->subject('Farm2Fork - TĂ i khoáº£n Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥p');
                }
            );
            $mailStatus = 'SENT';
            $mailMessage = 'Email hÆ°á»›ng dáº«n Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»­i.';
        }

        return redirect()->route('admin.registrations.show', $applicationCode)
            ->with('status', 'Cáº¥p tĂ i khoáº£n thĂ nh cĂ´ng. '.$mailMessage)
            ->with('provisioning_info', [
                'email' => $created->email,
                'mail_status' => $mailStatus,
            ]);
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\RegistrationApprovalController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Services\OrganizationRegistrationService;
use App\Support\RegistrationStatuses;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class RegistrationApprovalController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private OrganizationRegistrationService $service)
    {
    }

    public function index(Request $request): View
    {
        $registrations = $this->service->getAll($request->only(['status', 'q', 'type']));

        return view('admin.registrations.index', [
            'registrations' => $registrations,
            'statusOptions' => RegistrationStatuses::labels(),
            'filters' => $request->only(['status', 'q', 'type']),
        ]);
    }

    public function show(string $applicationCode): View
    {
        $registration = $this->service->findByCode($applicationCode);

        abort_unless($registration, 404);

        return view('admin.registrations.show', [
            'registration' => $registration,
            'statusOptions' => RegistrationStatuses::labels(),
        ]);
    }

    public function verifyContact(Request $request, string $applicationCode): RedirectResponse
    {
        $updated = $this->service->markContactVerified($applicationCode, (int) $request->user()->getAuthIdentifier());

        abort_unless($updated, 404);

        return redirect()
            ->route('admin.registrations.show', $applicationCode)
            ->with('status', 'ÄĂ£ xĂ¡c thá»±c thĂ´ng tin liĂªn há»‡ cho há»“ sÆ¡.');
    }

    public function approve(Request $request, string $applicationCode): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'note' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ]);

        $note = $request->string('note')->toString();
        if ($note === '') {
            $note = 'Há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘áº¡t yĂªu cáº§u vĂ  Ä‘Æ°á»£c phĂª duyá»‡t.';
        }

        $updated = $this->service->updateStatus(
            $applicationCode,
            RegistrationStatuses::DA_PHE_DUYET,
            $note,
            (int) $request->user()->getAuthIdentifier()
        );

        abort_unless($updated, 404);

        return redirect()
            ->route('admin.registrations.show', $applicationCode)
            ->with('status', 'ÄĂ£ phĂª duyá»‡t há»“ sÆ¡. CĂ³ thá»ƒ tiáº¿n hĂ nh cáº¥p tĂ i khoáº£n.');
    }

    public function reject(Request $request, string $applicationCode): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'reason' => ['required', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'reason.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p lĂ½ do tá»« chá»‘i.',
        ]);

        $updated = $this->service->updateStatus(
            $applicationCode,
            RegistrationStatuses::TU_CHOI,
            $validated['reason'],
            (int) $request->user()->getAuthIdentifier()
        );

        abort_unless($updated, 404);

        return redirect()->route('admin.registrations.show', $applicationCode)
            ->with('status', 'Há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Ă£ bá»‹ tá»« chá»‘i.');
    }

    public function requestSupplement(Request $request, string $applicationCode): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'supplement_note' => ['required', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'supplement_note.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p ná»™i dung cáº§n bá»• sung.',
        ]);

        $updated = $this->service->updateStatus(
            $applicationCode,
            RegistrationStatuses::YEU_CAU_BO_SUNG,
            $validated['supplement_note'],
            (int) $request->user()->getAuthIdentifier()
        );

        abort_unless($updated, 404);

        return redirect()->route('admin.registrations.show', $applicationCode)
            ->with('status', 'ÄĂ£ gá»­i yĂªu cáº§u bá»• sung há»“ sÆ¡.');
    }

    public function downloadDocument(string $applicationCode, string $docId)
    {
        $path = $this->service->resolveDocumentPath($applicationCode, $docId);

        abort_unless($path && Storage::disk('local')->exists($path), 404);

        return Storage::disk('local')->download($path);
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\ThresholdController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ThresholdController extends Controller
{
    public function index(): View
    {
        $thresholds = DB::table('cau_hinh_nguong_canh_bao as ch')
            ->leftJoin('kho_lanh as k', 'k.kho_lanh_id', '=', 'ch.kho_lanh_id')
            ->select('ch.*', 'k.ten_kho')
            ->orderByDesc('ch.cau_hinh_id')
            ->paginate(20);

        $warehouses = DB::table('kho_lanh')->select('kho_lanh_id', 'ten_kho')->orderBy('ten_kho')->get();

        return view('admin.thresholds.index', [
            'thresholds' => $thresholds,
            'warehouses' => $warehouses,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'kho_lanh_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:kho_lanh,kho_lanh_id'],
            'nhiet_do_min' => ['required', 'numeric'],
            'nhiet_do_max' => ['required', 'numeric', 'gt:nhiet_do_min'],
            'do_am_min' => ['nullable', 'numeric'],
            'do_am_max' => ['nullable', 'numeric'],
            'thoi_gian_vuot_nguong_phut' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:1'],
        ], [
            'kho_lanh_id.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng chá»n kho láº¡nh.',
            'nhiet_do_max.gt' => 'Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ tá»‘i Ä‘a pháº£i lá»›n hÆ¡n nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ tá»‘i thiá»ƒu.',
        ]);

        DB::table('cau_hinh_nguong_canh_bao')->insert([
            'kho_lanh_id' => $validated['kho_lanh_id'],
            'nhiet_do_min' => $validated['nhiet_do_min'],
            'nhiet_do_max' => $validated['nhiet_do_max'],
            'do_am_min' => $validated['do_am_min'] ?? null,
            'do_am_max' => $validated['do_am_max'] ?? null,
            'thoi_gian_vuot_nguong_phut' => $validated['thoi_gian_vuot_nguong_phut'] ?? 15,
            'trang_thai' => 'HIEU_LUC',
            'mo_ta' => $request->string('mo_ta')->toString() ?: null,
        ]);

        return back()->with('status', 'ÄĂ£ thĂªm cáº¥u hĂ¬nh ngÆ°á»¡ng cáº£nh bĂ¡o.');
    }

    public function toggle(int $id): RedirectResponse
    {
        $row = DB::table('cau_hinh_nguong_canh_bao')->where('cau_hinh_id', $id)->first();
        abort_unless($row, 404);

        $next = ($row->trang_thai ?? '') === 'HIEU_LUC' ? 'NGUNG' : 'HIEU_LUC';
        DB::table('cau_hinh_nguong_canh_bao')->where('cau_hinh_id', $id)->update(['trang_thai' => $next]);

        return back()->with('status', 'ÄĂ£ cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thĂ¡i cáº¥u hĂ¬nh.');
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\ChangePasswordController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Support\InitialPasswordStore;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ChangePasswordController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private InitialPasswordStore $store)
    {
    }

    public function show(Request $request): View
    {
        return view('auth.change-password', [
            'mustChange' => $this->store->shouldForceChange((int) $request->user()->getAuthIdentifier()),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'current_password' => ['required', 'string'],
            'new_password' => [
                'required',
                'string',
                'confirmed',
                'min:10',
                'regex:/[A-Z]/',
                'regex:/[a-z]/',
                'regex:/[0-9]/',
                'regex:/[^A-Za-z0-9]/',
            ],
        ], [
            'current_password.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i.',
            'new_password.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u má»›i.',
            'new_password.confirmed' => 'XĂ¡c nháº­n máº­t kháº©u má»›i khĂ´ng khá»›p.',
            'new_password.min' => 'Máº­t kháº©u má»›i tá»‘i thiá»ƒu 10 kĂ½ tá»±.',
            'new_password.regex' => 'Máº­t kháº©u má»›i pháº£i cĂ³ chá»¯ hoa, chá»¯ thÆ°á»ng, sá»‘ vĂ  kĂ½ tá»± Ä‘áº·c biá»‡t.',
        ]);

        if (!Hash::check($validated['current_password'], $user->mat_khau_hash)) {
            return back()->withErrors([
                'current_password' => 'Máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i khĂ´ng Ä‘Ăºng.',
            ])->withInput();
        }

        $user->mat_khau_hash = Hash::make($validated['new_password']);
        $user->save();

        $this->store->clearForceChange((int) $user->getAuthIdentifier());

        return redirect()->route('dashboard')->with('status', 'Äá»•i máº­t kháº©u thĂ nh cĂ´ng.');
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\LoginController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Support\AccountStatus;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class LoginController extends Controller
{
    public function show(): View
    {
        return view('auth.login');
    }

    public function login(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $credentials = $request->validate([
            'login' => ['required', 'string', 'max:254'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ], [
            'login.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p tĂ i khoáº£n hoáº·c email.',
            'password.required' => 'Vui lĂ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u.',
        ]);

        $loginValue = trim($credentials['login']);
        $password = $credentials['password'];

        $attemptPayload = [
            'email' => $loginValue,
            'password' => $password,
        ];

        if (!Auth::attempt($attemptPayload, $request->boolean('remember'))) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'login' => 'ThĂ´ng tin Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p khĂ´ng Ä‘Ăºng hoáº·c tĂ i khoáº£n chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥p quyá»n truy cáº­p.',
            ]);
        }

        $user = Auth::user();

        if ($user && !in_array($user->trang_thai, AccountStatus::activeStatuses(), true)) {
            Auth::logout();
            $request->session()->invalidate();

            $message = $user->trang_thai === AccountStatus::CHO_KICH_HOAT
                ? 'TĂ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c kĂ­ch hoáº¡t. Vui lĂ²ng chá» phĂª duyá»‡t há»“ sÆ¡.'
                : 'TĂ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n Ä‘Ă£ bá»‹ khĂ³a. Vui lĂ²ng liĂªn há»‡ quáº£n trá»‹ viĂªn.';

            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['login' => $message]);
        }

        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard'));
    }

    public function logout(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect()->route('auth.login.form');
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Middleware\EnsureAccountIsActive.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use App\Support\AccountStatus;
use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class EnsureAccountIsActive
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user && !in_array($user->trang_thai, AccountStatus::activeStatuses(), true)) {
            Auth::logout();

            return redirect()->route('auth.login.form')
                ->withErrors(['login' => 'Tai khoan chua duoc kich hoat hoac dang bi khoa.']);
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Middleware\ForceChangePassword.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use App\Support\InitialPasswordStore;
use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class ForceChangePassword
{
    public function __construct(private InitialPasswordStore $store)
    {
    }

    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if (!$user) {
            return $next($request);
        }

        $isChangingPassword = $request->routeIs('auth.change-password.*');

        if ($this->store->shouldForceChange((int) $user->getAuthIdentifier()) && !$isChangingPassword) {
            return redirect()->route('auth.change-password.form')
                ->with('warning', 'Ban can doi mat khau ban dau truoc khi tiep tuc su dung he thong.');
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Middleware\RequireAdminRole.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class RequireAdminRole
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if (!$user || !$user->relationLoaded('vaiTro')) {
            $user?->load('vaiTro');
        }

        if (!$user || !$user->vaiTro || $user->vaiTro->ma_vai_tro !== 'ADMIN') {
            abort(403, 'Ban khong co quyen truy cap khu vuc quan tri.');
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Http\Middleware\RequireRole.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class RequireRole
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next, string ...$roles): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if (!$user || !$user->relationLoaded('vaiTro')) {
            $user?->load('vaiTro');
        }

        $role = $user?->vaiTro?->ma_vai_tro;

        if (!$role || ($roles !== [] && !in_array($role, $roles, true))) {
            abort(403, 'Báº¡n khĂ´ng cĂ³ quyá»n truy cáº­p chá»©c nÄƒng nĂ y.');
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}

class TaiKhoanHeThong extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasFactory;
    use Notifiable;

    protected $table = 'tai_khoan_he_thong';

    protected $primaryKey = 'tai_khoan_id';

    public $timestamps = true;

    protected $fillable = [
        'vai_tro_id',
        'to_chuc_id',
        'ho_ten',
        'email',
        'mat_khau_hash',
        'so_dien_thoai',
        'trang_thai',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'mat_khau_hash',
    ];

    public function getAuthPassword(): string
    {
        return $this->mat_khau_hash;
    }

    public function vaiTro()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(VaiTro::class, 'vai_tro_id', 'vai_tro_id');
    }

    public function toChuc()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(ToChuc::class, 'to_chuc_id', 'to_chuc_id');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

// use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;

class User extends Authenticatable
{
    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\UserFactory> */
    use HasFactory, Notifiable;

    /**
     * The attributes that are mass assignable.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'email',
        'password',
    ];

    /**
     * The attributes that should be hidden for serialization.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
    ];

    /**
     * Get the attributes that should be cast.
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
            'password' => 'hashed',
        ];
    }
}

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Services\OrganizationRegistrationService.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\ToChuc;
use App\Support\RegistrationStatuses;
use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class OrganizationRegistrationService
{
    private const ROOT_PATH = 'organization_registrations';

    public function create(array $input): array
    {
        $maHoSo = $this->generateApplicationCode();

        $toChuc = ToChuc::create([
            'ma_to_chuc' => $this->generateOrganizationCode($input['organization_type']),
            'ten_to_chuc' => $input['organization_name'],
            'loai_to_chuc' => $input['organization_type'],
            'ma_so_thue' => $input['legal_id'],
            'dia_chi' => $input['organization_address'],
            'email' => $input['contact_email'],
            'so_dien_thoai' => $input['contact_phone'],
            'trang_thai' => RegistrationStatuses::CHO_XAC_THUC,
        ]);

        $filePayload = $this->storeFiles(
            $maHoSo,
            $input['legal_documents'] ?? [],
            $input['certification_documents'] ?? []
        );

        $this->saveMeta($maHoSo, [
            'application_code' => $maHoSo,
            'organization_id' => $toChuc->to_chuc_id,
            'status' => RegistrationStatuses::CHO_XAC_THUC,
            'organization' => [
                'type' => $input['organization_type'],
                'name' => $input['organization_name'],
                'representative' => $input['representative_name'],
                'legal_id' => $input['legal_id'],
                'contact_phone' => $input['contact_phone'],
                'contact_email' => $input['contact_email'],
                'address' => $input['organization_address'],
                'cultivation_region' => $input['cultivation_region'],
            ],
            'infrastructure' => [
                'cold_chain_items' => $input['cold_chain_items'] ?? [],
                'sensors' => $input['sensors'] ?? [],
            ],
            'documents' => $filePayload,
            'contact_verification' => [
                'mode' => config('app.env') === 'local' ? 'SIMULATED' : 'UNCONFIGURED',
                'verified' => false,
                'last_message' => config('app.env') === 'local'
                    ? 'Há»‡ thá»‘ng Ä‘ang á»Ÿ cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ mĂ´ phá»ng xĂ¡c thá»±c liĂªn há»‡ trong mĂ´i trÆ°á»ng phĂ¡t triá»ƒn.'
                    : 'ChÆ°a cáº¥u hĂ¬nh kĂªnh email/OTP thá»±c táº¿.',
            ],
            'history' => [
                [
                    'status' => RegistrationStatuses::CHO_XAC_THUC,
                    'note' => 'Há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Æ°á»£c táº¡o má»›i.',
                    'by' => 'PUBLIC_USER',
                    'at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
                ],
            ],
            'approval' => [
                'approved_by_account_id' => null,
                'approved_at' => null,
                'reject_reason' => null,
                'supplement_request' => null,
            ],
            'account_provisioning' => [
                'provisioned' => false,
                'provisioned_account_id' => null,
                'provisioned_at' => null,
            ],
            'created_at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
            'updated_at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
        ]);

        return [
            'application_code' => $maHoSo,
            'organization_id' => $toChuc->to_chuc_id,
            'status' => RegistrationStatuses::CHO_XAC_THUC,
        ];
    }

    public function getAll(array $filters = []): array
    {
        $rows = [];

        foreach (Storage::disk('local')->files(self::ROOT_PATH) as $filePath) {
            if (!str_ends_with($filePath, '.json')) {
                continue;
            }

            $payload = $this->readMetaByPath($filePath);

            if (!$payload) {
                continue;
            }

            $rows[] = $payload;
        }

        usort($rows, fn ($a, $b) => strcmp($b['updated_at'] ?? '', $a['updated_at'] ?? ''));

        return array_values(array_filter($rows, function (array $item) use ($filters) {
            $status = $filters['status'] ?? null;
            $keyword = trim((string) ($filters['q'] ?? ''));
            $type = $filters['type'] ?? null;

            if ($status && $item['status'] !== $status) {
                return false;
            }

            if ($type && ($item['organization']['type'] ?? null) !== $type) {
                return false;
            }

            if ($keyword !== '') {
                $haystack = mb_strtolower(implode(' ', [
                    $item['application_code'] ?? '',
                    $item['organization']['name'] ?? '',
                    $item['organization']['representative'] ?? '',
                    $item['organization']['type'] ?? '',
                ]));

                if (!str_contains($haystack, mb_strtolower($keyword))) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }));
    }

    public function findByCode(string $applicationCode): ?array
    {
        $path = $this->metaPath($applicationCode);

        if (!Storage::disk('local')->exists($path)) {
            return null;
        }

        return $this->readMetaByPath($path);
    }

    public function updateStatus(string $applicationCode, string $status, string $note, int $actorAccountId): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->findByCode($applicationCode);

        if (!$payload) {
            return null;
        }

        $payload['status'] = $status;
        $payload['updated_at'] = now()->toDateTimeString();
        $payload['history'][] = [
            'status' => $status,
            'note' => $note,
            'by' => $actorAccountId,
            'at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
        ];

        if ($status === RegistrationStatuses::DA_PHE_DUYET) {
            $payload['approval']['approved_by_account_id'] = $actorAccountId;
            $payload['approval']['approved_at'] = now()->toDateTimeString();
            $payload['approval']['reject_reason'] = null;
            $payload['approval']['supplement_request'] = null;
        }

        if ($status === RegistrationStatuses::TU_CHOI) {
            $payload['approval']['reject_reason'] = $note;
        }

        if ($status === RegistrationStatuses::YEU_CAU_BO_SUNG) {
            $payload['approval']['supplement_request'] = $note;
        }

        ToChuc::where('to_chuc_id', $payload['organization_id'])->update([
            'trang_thai' => $status,
        ]);

        $this->saveMeta($applicationCode, $payload);

        return $payload;
    }

    public function markContactVerified(string $applicationCode, int $actorAccountId): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->findByCode($applicationCode);

        if (!$payload) {
            return null;
        }

        $payload['contact_verification']['verified'] = true;
        $payload['contact_verification']['verified_at'] = now()->toDateTimeString();
        $payload['contact_verification']['verified_by'] = $actorAccountId;
        $payload['contact_verification']['last_message'] = 'ThĂ´ng tin liĂªn há»‡ Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xĂ¡c thá»±c bá»Ÿi quáº£n trá»‹ viĂªn.';

        if ($payload['status'] === RegistrationStatuses::CHO_XAC_THUC) {
            $payload['status'] = RegistrationStatuses::CHO_DUYET;
            ToChuc::where('to_chuc_id', $payload['organization_id'])->update([
                'trang_thai' => RegistrationStatuses::CHO_DUYET,
            ]);
            $payload['history'][] = [
                'status' => RegistrationStatuses::CHO_DUYET,
                'note' => 'ÄĂ£ xĂ¡c thá»±c thĂ´ng tin liĂªn há»‡. Chuyá»ƒn sang chá» duyá»‡t há»“ sÆ¡.',
                'by' => $actorAccountId,
                'at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
            ];
        }

        $payload['updated_at'] = now()->toDateTimeString();
        $this->saveMeta($applicationCode, $payload);

        return $payload;
    }

    public function markProvisioned(string $applicationCode, int $accountId): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->findByCode($applicationCode);

        if (!$payload) {
            return null;
        }

        $payload['status'] = RegistrationStatuses::DA_CAP_TAI_KHOAN;
        $payload['account_provisioning'] = [
            'provisioned' => true,
            'provisioned_account_id' => $accountId,
            'provisioned_at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
        ];
        $payload['updated_at'] = now()->toDateTimeString();
        $payload['history'][] = [
            'status' => RegistrationStatuses::DA_CAP_TAI_KHOAN,
            'note' => 'TĂ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥p sau phĂª duyá»‡t.',
            'by' => $accountId,
            'at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
        ];

        ToChuc::where('to_chuc_id', $payload['organization_id'])->update([
            'trang_thai' => RegistrationStatuses::DA_CAP_TAI_KHOAN,
        ]);

        $this->saveMeta($applicationCode, $payload);

        return $payload;
    }

    public function resolveDocumentPath(string $applicationCode, string $key): ?string
    {
        $payload = $this->findByCode($applicationCode);

        if (!$payload) {
            return null;
        }

        foreach (['legal_documents', 'certification_documents'] as $group) {
            foreach (($payload['documents'][$group] ?? []) as $doc) {
                if (($doc['id'] ?? null) === $key) {
                    return $doc['path'] ?? null;
                }
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    private function storeFiles(string $applicationCode, array $legalFiles, array $certificateFiles): array
    {
        return [
            'legal_documents' => $this->storeFileSet($applicationCode, 'legal_documents', $legalFiles),
            'certification_documents' => $this->storeFileSet($applicationCode, 'certification_documents', $certificateFiles),
        ];
    }

    private function storeFileSet(string $applicationCode, string $group, array $files): array
    {
        $result = [];

        foreach ($files as $file) {
            if (!$file instanceof UploadedFile) {
                continue;
            }

            $path = $file->storeAs(
                self::ROOT_PATH.'/'.$applicationCode.'/'.$group,
                now()->format('YmdHis').'_'.Str::slug(pathinfo($file->getClientOriginalName(), PATHINFO_FILENAME)).'.'.$file->getClientOriginalExtension(),
                'local'
            );

            $result[] = [
                'id' => (string) Str::uuid(),
                'name' => $file->getClientOriginalName(),
                'mime' => $file->getClientMimeType(),
                'size' => $file->getSize(),
                'path' => $path,
                'uploaded_at' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
            ];
        }

        return $result;
    }

    private function readMetaByPath(string $path): ?array
    {
        $raw = Storage::disk('local')->get($path);
        $data = json_decode($raw, true);

        return is_array($data) ? $data : null;
    }

    private function saveMeta(string $applicationCode, array $payload): void
    {
        Storage::disk('local')->put(
            $this->metaPath($applicationCode),
            json_encode($payload, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)
        );
    }

    private function metaPath(string $applicationCode): string
    {
        return self::ROOT_PATH.'/'.$applicationCode.'.json';
    }

    private function generateApplicationCode(): string
    {
        return 'HS-'.now()->format('Ymd').'-'.strtoupper(Str::random(6));
    }

    private function generateOrganizationCode(string $organizationType): string
    {
        $prefix = match ($organizationType) {
            'NONG_HO' => 'NCC-NH-',
            'HTX' => 'NCC-HTX-',
            'DOANH_NGHIEP_NONG_NGHIEP' => 'NCC-DN-',
            default => 'NCC-ORG-',
        };

        return $prefix.strtoupper(Str::random(6));
    }
}

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\app\Support\InitialPasswordStore.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Support;

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class InitialPasswordStore
{
    private const FILE_PATH = 'auth_meta/initial_password_flags.json';

    public function shouldForceChange(int $accountId): bool
    {
        $flags = $this->load();

        return (bool) ($flags[$accountId] ?? false);
    }

    public function markForceChange(int $accountId): void
    {
        $flags = $this->load();
        $flags[$accountId] = true;
        $this->save($flags);
    }

    public function clearForceChange(int $accountId): void
    {
        $flags = $this->load();
        $flags[$accountId] = false;
        $this->save($flags);
    }

    private function load(): array
    {
        if (!Storage::disk('local')->exists(self::FILE_PATH)) {
            return [];
        }

        $raw = Storage::disk('local')->get(self::FILE_PATH);
        $decoded = json_decode($raw, true);

        return is_array($decoded) ? $decoded : [];
    }

    private function save(array $flags): void
    {
        Storage::disk('local')->put(self::FILE_PATH, json_encode($flags, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE));
    }
}

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\bootstrap\app.php =====
<?php

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Exceptions;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Middleware;

return Application::configure(basePath: dirname(__DIR__))
    ->withRouting(
        web: __DIR__.'/../routes/web.php',
        commands: __DIR__.'/../routes/console.php',
        health: '/up',
    )
    ->withMiddleware(function (Middleware $middleware) {
        $middleware->redirectGuestsTo(fn () => route('auth.login.form'));
        $middleware->alias([
            'admin.role' => App\Http\Middleware\RequireAdminRole::class,
            'role' => App\Http\Middleware\RequireRole::class,
            'active.account' => App\Http\Middleware\EnsureAccountIsActive::class,
            'force.change.password' => App\Http\Middleware\ForceChangePassword::class,
        ]);
    })
    ->withExceptions(function (Exceptions $exceptions) {
        //
    })->create();

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\config\session.php =====
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Default Session Driver
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines the default session driver that is utilized for
    | incoming requests. Laravel supports a variety of storage options to
    | persist session data. Database storage is a great default choice.
    |
    | Supported: "file", "cookie", "database", "apc",
    |            "memcached", "redis", "dynamodb", "array"
    |
    */

    'driver' => env('SESSION_DRIVER', 'database'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Lifetime
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may specify the number of minutes that you wish the session
    | to be allowed to remain idle before it expires. If you want them
    | to expire immediately when the browser is closed then you may
    | indicate that via the expire_on_close configuration option.
    |
    */

    'lifetime' => (int) env('SESSION_LIFETIME', 120),

    'expire_on_close' => env('SESSION_EXPIRE_ON_CLOSE', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Encryption
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option allows you to easily specify that all of your session data
    | should be encrypted before it's stored. All encryption is performed
    | automatically by Laravel and you may use the session like normal.
    |
    */

    'encrypt' => env('SESSION_ENCRYPT', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session File Location
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When utilizing the "file" session driver, the session files are placed
    | on disk. The default storage location is defined here; however, you
    | are free to provide another location where they should be stored.
    |
    */

    'files' => storage_path('framework/sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Connection
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" or "redis" session drivers, you may specify a
    | connection that should be used to manage these sessions. This should
    | correspond to a connection in your database configuration options.
    |
    */

    'connection' => env('SESSION_CONNECTION'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Table
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" session driver, you may specify the table to
    | be used to store sessions. Of course, a sensible default is defined
    | for you; however, you're welcome to change this to another table.
    |
    */

    'table' => env('SESSION_TABLE', 'sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cache Store
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using one of the framework's cache driven session backends, you may
    | define the cache store which should be used to store the session data
    | between requests. This must match one of your defined cache stores.
    |
    | Affects: "apc", "dynamodb", "memcached", "redis"
    |
    */

    'store' => env('SESSION_STORE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Sweeping Lottery
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Some session drivers must manually sweep their storage location to get
    | rid of old sessions from storage. Here are the chances that it will
    | happen on a given request. By default, the odds are 2 out of 100.
    |
    */

    'lottery' => [2, 100],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Name
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may change the name of the session cookie that is created by
    | the framework. Typically, you should not need to change this value
    | since doing so does not grant a meaningful security improvement.
    |
    */

    'cookie' => env(
        'SESSION_COOKIE',
        Str::slug(env('APP_NAME', 'laravel'), '_').'_session'
    ),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Path
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | The session cookie path determines the path for which the cookie will
    | be regarded as available. Typically, this will be the root path of
    | your application, but you're free to change this when necessary.
    |
    */

    'path' => env('SESSION_PATH', '/'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Domain
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This value determines the domain and subdomains the session cookie is
    | available to. By default, the cookie will be available to the root
    | domain and all subdomains. Typically, this shouldn't be changed.
    |
    */

    'domain' => env('SESSION_DOMAIN'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTPS Only Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | By setting this option to true, session cookies will only be sent back
    | to the server if the browser has a HTTPS connection. This will keep
    | the cookie from being sent to you when it can't be done securely.
    |
    */

    'secure' => env('SESSION_SECURE_COOKIE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTP Access Only
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will prevent JavaScript from accessing the
    | value of the cookie and the cookie will only be accessible through
    | the HTTP protocol. It's unlikely you should disable this option.
    |
    */

    'http_only' => env('SESSION_HTTP_ONLY', true),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Same-Site Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines how your cookies behave when cross-site requests
    | take place, and can be used to mitigate CSRF attacks. By default, we
    | will set this value to "lax" to permit secure cross-site requests.
    |
    | See: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Headers/Set-Cookie#samesitesamesite-value
    |
    | Supported: "lax", "strict", "none", null
    |
    */

    'same_site' => env('SESSION_SAME_SITE', 'lax'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Partitioned Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will tie the cookie to the top-level site for
    | a cross-site context. Partitioned cookies are accepted by the browser
    | when flagged "secure" and the Same-Site attribute is set to "none".
    |
    */

    'partitioned' => env('SESSION_PARTITIONED_COOKIE', false),

];



===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\database\factories\UserFactory.php =====
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * @extends \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory<\App\Models\User>
 */
class UserFactory extends Factory
{
    /**
     * The current password being used by the factory.
     */
    protected static ?string $password;

    /**
     * Define the model's default state.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => fake()->name(),
            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
            'email_verified_at' => now(),
            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
     */
    public function unverified(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
            'email_verified_at' => null,
        ]);
    }
}

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\resources\js\app.js =====
import './bootstrap';
document.querySelectorAll('[data-toggle-password]').forEach((button) => {
    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const targetId = button.getAttribute('data-toggle-password');
        const input = document.getElementById(targetId);

        if (!input) {
            return;
        }

        const asText = input.getAttribute('type') === 'password';
        input.setAttribute('type', asText ? 'text' : 'password');
        button.textContent = asText ? 'áº¨n' : 'Hiá»‡n';
    });
});

document.querySelectorAll('input[type="file"][data-preview-target]').forEach((input) => {
    input.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const targetId = input.getAttribute('data-preview-target');
        const target = targetId ? document.getElementById(targetId) : null;

        if (!target) {
            return;
        }

        target.innerHTML = '';
        if (input.files.length === 0) {
            return;
        }
        Array.from(input.files || []).forEach((file) => {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.textContent = `âœ“ ${file.name} (${Math.ceil(file.size / 1024)} KB) â€” Ä‘Ă£ chá»n`;
            target.appendChild(row);
        });
    });
});

const coldChainWrapper = document.getElementById('cold-chain-wrapper');
const sensorWrapper = document.getElementById('sensor-wrapper');

function buildItem(prefix, idx, label, fields) {
    const item = document.createElement('div');
    item.className = 'f2f-item';

    const title = document.createElement('div');
    title.className = 'f2f-item-head';

    const name = document.createElement('strong');
    name.textContent = `${label} #${idx + 1}`;
    title.appendChild(name);

    const remove = document.createElement('button');
    remove.type = 'button';
    remove.className = 'f2f-item-remove';
    remove.textContent = 'âœ• XĂ³a';
    remove.addEventListener('click', () => item.remove());
    title.appendChild(remove);

    const grid = document.createElement('div');
    grid.className = 'f2f-grid-two';
    grid.style.marginTop = '10px';
    fields.forEach(([key, placeholder]) => {
        const field = document.createElement('input');
        field.className = 'f2f-input';
        field.name = `${prefix}[${idx}][${key}]`;
        field.placeholder = placeholder;
        grid.appendChild(field);
    });

    item.appendChild(title);
    item.appendChild(grid);
    return item;
}

const COLD_CHAIN_FIELDS = [
    ['name', 'TĂªn kho / tá»§ Ä‘Ă´ng / phÆ°Æ¡ng tiá»‡n'],
    ['type', 'Loáº¡i (kho láº¡nh, xe Ä‘Ă´ng láº¡nh...)'],
    ['identifier', 'MĂ£ Ä‘á»‹nh danh thiáº¿t bá»‹'],
    ['description', 'MĂ´ táº£ thĂªm (sá»©c chá»©a, tuyáº¿n...)'],
];

const SENSOR_FIELDS = [
    ['name', 'TĂªn cáº£m biáº¿n / GPS tracker'],
    ['type', 'Loáº¡i (nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™, Ä‘á»™ áº©m, GPS...)'],
    ['serial', 'Serial / IMEI hoáº·c Ä‘á»‹nh danh'],
    ['description', 'MĂ´ táº£ vá»‹ trĂ­ láº¯p Ä‘áº·t'],
];

function itemCount(wrapper) {
    return wrapper ? wrapper.querySelectorAll('.f2f-item').length : 0;
}

function removePlaceholder(wrapper) {
    wrapper?.querySelector('.f2f-empty')?.remove();
}

document.querySelector('[data-add-cold-chain]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!coldChainWrapper) {
        return;
    }
    removePlaceholder(coldChainWrapper);
    const idx = itemCount(coldChainWrapper);
    coldChainWrapper.appendChild(buildItem('cold_chain_items', idx, 'Háº¡ táº§ng láº¡nh', COLD_CHAIN_FIELDS));
});

document.querySelector('[data-add-sensor]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!sensorWrapper) {
        return;
    }
    removePlaceholder(sensorWrapper);
    const idx = itemCount(sensorWrapper);
    sensorWrapper.appendChild(buildItem('sensors', idx, 'Cáº£m biáº¿n/GPS', SENSOR_FIELDS));
});

===== FILE: C:\laravel\Farm2Fork\routes\web.php =====
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AccountController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AccountProvisionController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\RegistrationApprovalController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\ThresholdController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\ChangePasswordController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\LoginController;
use App\Http\Controllers\DashboardController;
use App\Http\Controllers\OrganizationRegistrationController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::get('/', function () {
    return redirect()->route('auth.login.form');
});

Route::middleware('guest')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/login', [LoginController::class, 'show'])->name('auth.login.form');
    Route::post('/login', [LoginController::class, 'login'])->name('auth.login.submit');

    Route::get('/register-organization', [OrganizationRegistrationController::class, 'showForm'])
        ->name('organization-registration.form');
    Route::post('/register-organization', [OrganizationRegistrationController::class, 'submit'])
        ->name('organization-registration.submit');
    Route::get('/register-organization/success/{applicationCode}', [OrganizationRegistrationController::class, 'success'])
        ->name('organization-registration.show');
});

Route::middleware(['auth', 'active.account'])->group(function () {
    Route::post('/logout', [LoginController::class, 'logout'])->name('auth.logout');

    Route::get('/change-password', [ChangePasswordController::class, 'show'])
        ->name('auth.change-password.form');
    Route::put('/change-password', [ChangePasswordController::class, 'update'])
        ->name('auth.change-password.update');

    Route::middleware('force.change.password')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/dashboard', DashboardController::class)->name('dashboard');

        Route::prefix('admin/registrations')
            ->name('admin.registrations.')
            ->middleware('admin.role')
            ->group(function () {
                Route::get('/', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'index'])->name('index');
                Route::get('/{applicationCode}', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'show'])->name('show');
                Route::post('/{applicationCode}/verify-contact', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'verifyContact'])->name('verify-contact');
                Route::post('/{applicationCode}/approve', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'approve'])->name('approve');
                Route::post('/{applicationCode}/reject', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'reject'])->name('reject');
                Route::post('/{applicationCode}/request-supplement', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'requestSupplement'])->name('request-supplement');
                Route::post('/{applicationCode}/provision-account', [AccountProvisionController::class, 'provision'])->name('provision-account');
                Route::get('/{applicationCode}/documents/{docId}', [RegistrationApprovalController::class, 'downloadDocument'])->name('documents.download');
            });

        Route::prefix('admin/accounts')
            ->name('admin.accounts.')
            ->middleware('admin.role')
            ->group(function () {
                Route::get('/', [AccountController::class, 'index'])->name('index');
                Route::post('/{id}/lock', [AccountController::class, 'lock'])->name('lock');
                Route::post('/{id}/unlock', [AccountController::class, 'unlock'])->name('unlock');
                Route::post('/{id}/role', [AccountController::class, 'changeRole'])->name('change-role');
            });

        Route::prefix('admin/thresholds')
            ->name('admin.thresholds.')
            ->middleware('admin.role')
            ->group(function () {
                Route::get('/', [ThresholdController::class, 'index'])->name('index');
                Route::post('/', [ThresholdController::class, 'store'])->name('store');
                Route::post('/{id}/toggle', [ThresholdController::class, 'toggle'])->name('toggle');
            });
    });
});
